max_iter=10000, polish=True)","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nDC-OPF with Reserve Co-optimization for Market Clearing Analysis\n\nThis script performs market clearing optimization for a power system,\ncomparing a base case with a counterfactual scenario where a specific\ntransmission line capacity is increased by 20%.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport cvxpy as cp\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Tuple\n\n\ndef load_network_data(filename: str) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER network data from JSON file\"\"\"\n    with open(filename, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    return data\n\n\ndef solve_dcopf_with_reserves(\n    buses: np.ndarray,\n    generators: np.ndarray,\n    branches: np.ndarray,\n    gencosts: np.ndarray,\n    reserve_capacity: List[float],\n    reserve_requirement: float,\n    baseMVA: float = 100.0,\n    line_capacity_adjustments: Dict[Tuple[int, int], float] = None\n) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF with reserve co-optimization\n\n    Parameters:\n    - buses: Bus data matrix (MATPOWER format)\n    - generators: Generator data matrix\n    - branches: Branch data matrix\n    - gencosts: Generator cost data\n    - reserve_capacity: Maximum reserve capacity per generator (MW)\n    - reserve_requirement: Minimum system reserve requirement (MW)\n    - baseMVA: Base MVA for per-unit system\n    - line_capacity_adjustments: Dict of {(from_bus, to_bus): capacity_multiplier}\n\n    Returns:\n    - Dictionary with optimization results\n    \"\"\"\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(generators)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    print(f\"Solving DC-OPF with reserves:\")\n    print(f\"  Buses: {n_bus}, Generators: {n_gen}, Branches: {n_branch}\")\n    print(f\"  Reserve requirement: {reserve_requirement:.1f} MW\")\n\n    # Decision variables\n    Pg = cp.Variable(n_gen, name=\"Pg\")  # Generator power output\n    R = cp.Variable(n_gen, name=\"R\")    # Reserve provision\n    theta = cp.Variable(n_bus, name=\"theta\")  # Voltage angles\n\n    # Build cost function\n    energy_cost_terms = []\n    reserve_cost_terms = []\n\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        # Energy cost (quadratic: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0)\n        if gencosts[i][0] == 2:  # Polynomial model\n            ncost = int(gencosts[i][3])\n            if ncost == 3:\n                c2 = gencosts[i][4]\n                c1 = gencosts[i][5]\n                c0 = gencosts[i][6]\n                energy_cost_terms.append(c2 * cp.power(Pg[i], 2) + c1 * Pg[i] + c0)\n            elif ncost == 2:\n                c1 = gencosts[i][4]\n                c0 = gencosts[i][5]\n                energy_cost_terms.append(c1 * Pg[i] + c0)\n            else:\n                print(f\"Warning: Unexpected ncost={ncost} for generator {i}\")\n\n        # Reserve cost (typically 10% of marginal energy cost)\n        marginal_cost = gencosts[i][5] if gencosts[i][0] == 2 else 0\n        reserve_price = marginal_cost * 0.1  # 10% of energy marginal cost\n        reserve_cost_terms.append(reserve_price * R[i])\n\n    total_cost = cp.sum(energy_cost_terms) + cp.sum(reserve_cost_terms)\n\n    # Constraints\n    constraints = []\n\n    # 1. Generator limits\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if generators[i][7] == 1:  # Generator is in service\n            Pmin = generators[i][9]\n            Pmax = generators[i][8]\n            constraints.append(Pg[i] >= Pmin)\n            constraints.append(Pg[i] <= Pmax)\n        else:\n            constraints.append(Pg[i] == 0)\n\n    # 2. Reserve constraints\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        # Non-negativity\n        constraints.append(R[i] >= 0)\n\n        # Reserve capacity limit\n        if i < len(reserve_capacity):\n            constraints.append(R[i] <= reserve_capacity[i])\n        else:\n            constraints.append(R[i] <= 0)\n\n        # Energy-reserve coupling (headroom constraint)\n        if generators[i][7] == 1:\n            Pmax = generators[i][8]\n            constraints.append(Pg[i] + R[i] <= Pmax)\n\n    # 3. System reserve requirement\n    reserve_constraint = cp.sum(R) >= reserve_requirement\n    constraints.append(reserve_constraint)\n\n    # 4. Build branch flow matrices\n    # For DC power flow: Pf = B * (theta_from - theta_to)\n    from_bus = np.zeros(n_branch, dtype=int)\n    to_bus = np.zeros(n_branch, dtype=int)\n    B_branch = np.zeros(n_branch)  # Branch susceptance\n    thermal_limits = np.zeros(n_branch)\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        from_bus[i] = int(branches[i][0]) - 1  # Convert to 0-indexed\n        to_bus[i] = int(branches[i][1]) - 1\n        X = branches[i][3]  # Reactance\n        if X != 0:\n            B_branch[i] = 1.0 / X\n        else:\n            B_branch[i] = 0.0\n\n        # Thermal limit (RATE_A)\n        thermal_limits[i] = branches[i][5]\n\n        # Apply capacity adjustment if specified\n        if line_capacity_adjustments:\n            from_bus_orig = int(branches[i][0])\n            to_bus_orig = int(branches[i][1])\n            if (from_bus_orig, to_bus_orig) in line_capacity_adjustments:\n                multiplier = line_capacity_adjustments[(from_bus_orig, to_bus_orig)]\n                thermal_limits[i] *= multiplier\n                print(f\"  Adjusting line {from_bus_orig}->{to_bus_orig}: \" +\n                      f\"{branches[i][5]:.1f} -> {thermal_limits[i]:.1f} MW\")\n            elif (to_bus_orig, from_bus_orig) in line_capacity_adjustments:\n                multiplier = line_capacity_adjustments[(to_bus_orig, from_bus_orig)]\n                thermal_limits[i] *= multiplier\n                print(f\"  Adjusting line {to_bus_orig}->{from_bus_orig}: \" +\n                      f\"{branches[i][5]:.1f} -> {thermal_limits[i]:.1f} MW\")\n\n    # 5. Power balance constraints at each bus\n    power_balance_constraints = []\n\n    for n in range(n_bus):\n        # Generation at this bus\n        gen_at_bus = 0\n        for i in range(n_gen):\n            if int(generators[i][0]) - 1 == n:\n                gen_at_bus += Pg[i]\n\n        # Demand at this bus\n        demand = buses[n][2]\n\n        # Net injection = generation - demand\n        net_injection = gen_at_bus - demand\n\n        # Flow out of bus (using DC power flow)\n        flow_out = 0\n        for l in range(n_branch):\n            if branches[l][10] == 1:  # Branch is in service\n                if from_bus[l] == n:\n                    # Flow out on lines starting from this bus\n                    flow_out += B_branch[l] * (theta[n] - theta[to_bus[l]])\n                elif to_bus[l] == n:\n                    # Flow in on lines ending at this bus (negative flow out)\n                    flow_out -= B_branch[l] * (theta[from_bus[l]] - theta[n])\n\n        # Power balance: net injection = flow out\n        constraint = (net_injection == flow_out)\n        power_balance_constraints.append(constraint)\n        constraints.append(constraint)\n\n    # 6. Transmission line thermal limits\n    line_flow_constraints = []\n    for l in range(n_branch):\n        if branches[l][10] == 1:  # Branch is in service\n            flow = B_branch[l] * (theta[from_bus[l]] - theta[to_bus[l]])\n\n            if thermal_limits[l] > 0:\n                # Bidirectional limit\n                constraints.append(flow <= thermal_limits[l])\n                constraints.append(flow >= -thermal_limits[l])\n                line_flow_constraints.append(flow)\n            else:\n                line_flow_constraints.append(flow)\n        else:\n            line_flow_constraints.append(0)\n\n    # 7. Reference angle (slack bus)\n    slack_bus = 0\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if buses[i][1] == 3:  # Slack bus type\n            slack_bus = i\n            break\n    constraints.append(theta[slack_bus] == 0)\n\n    # Solve the optimization problem\n    problem = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(total_cost), constraints)\n\n    print(\"  Solving optimization problem...\")\n    try:\n        problem.solve(solver=cp.SCS, max_iters=2500, verbose=False, eps=1e-4)\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"  SCS solver failed: {e}\")\n        print(\"  Trying OSQP solver...\")\n        problem.solve(solver=cp.OSQP, verbose=False, eps_abs=1e-4, eps_rel=1e-4)\n\n    if problem.status not in [cp.OPTIMAL, cp.OPTIMAL_INACCURATE]:\n        print(f\"  Warning: Solver status = {problem.status}\")\n\n    # Extract results\n    lmps = np.array([c.dual_value if c.dual_value is not None else 0.0\n                     for c in power_balance_constraints])\n\n    reserve_mcp = reserve_constraint.dual_value if reserve_constraint.dual_value else 0.0\n\n    # Calculate actual line flows\n    branch_flows = []\n    for l in range(n_branch):\n        if branches[l][10] == 1:\n            flow = B_branch[l] * (theta.value[from_bus[l]] - theta.value[to_bus[l]])\n            branch_flows.append(flow)\n        else:\n            branch_flows.append(0.0)\n\n    # Identify binding lines (>= 99% of capacity)\n    binding_lines = []\n    for l in range(n_branch):\n        if thermal_limits[l] > 0:\n            utilization = abs(branch_flows[l]) / thermal_limits[l]\n            if utilization >= 0.99:\n                binding_lines.append({\n                    'from': int(branches[l][0]),\n                    'to': int(branches[l][1]),\n                    'flow_MW': float(branch_flows[l]),\n                    'limit_MW': float(thermal_limits[l]),\n                    'utilization': float(utilization)\n                })\n\n    print(f\"  Status: {problem.status}\")\n    print(f\"  Total cost: ${problem.value:,.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"  Reserve MCP: ${reserve_mcp:.2f}/MWh\")\n    print(f\"  Binding lines: {len(binding_lines)}\")\n\n    return {\n        'status': problem.status,\n        'total_cost': problem.value,\n        'Pg': Pg.value,\n        'R': R.value,\n        'theta': theta.value,\n        'lmps': lmps,\n        'reserve_mcp': reserve_mcp,\n        'branch_flows': branch_flows,\n        'binding_lines': binding_lines,\n        'power_balance_constraints': power_balance_constraints\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main analysis workflow\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"Market Clearing Analysis: Base vs. Counterfactual Scenario\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # Load network data\n    print(\"\\nLoading network data...\")\n    data = load_network_data('network.json')\n\n    buses = np.array(data['bus'])\n    generators = np.array(data['gen'])\n    branches = np.array(data['branch'])\n    gencosts = np.array(data['gencost'])\n    reserve_capacity = data['reserve_capacity']\n    reserve_requirement = data['reserve_requirement']\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n\n    print(f\"Network loaded:\")\n    print(f\"  Buses: {len(buses)}\")\n    print(f\"  Generators: {len(generators)}\")\n    print(f\"  Branches: {len(branches)}\")\n    print(f\"  Total demand: {sum(bus[2] for bus in buses):.1f} MW\")\n    print(f\"  Reserve requirement: {reserve_requirement:.1f} MW\")\n\n    # Find the target line (64 -> 1501)\n    target_line_idx = None\n    for i, branch in enumerate(branches):\n        if (int(branch[0]) == 64 and int(branch[1]) == 1501) or \\\n           (int(branch[0]) == 1501 and int(branch[1]) == 64):\n            target_line_idx = i\n            break\n\n    if target_line_idx is None:\n        print(\"\\nError: Could not find line between bus 64 and 1501\")\n        return\n\n    target_from = int(branches[target_line_idx][0])\n    target_to = int(branches[target_line_idx][1])\n    base_capacity = branches[target_line_idx][5]\n\n    print(f\"\\nTarget line: {target_from} -> {target_to}\")\n    print(f\"  Base capacity: {base_capacity:.1f} MW\")\n    print(f\"  Counterfactual capacity: {base_capacity * 1.2:.1f} MW (+20%)\")\n\n    # Perform impact analysis\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"IMPACT ANALYSIS\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    cost_reduction = base_result['total_cost'] - cf_result['total_cost']\n    print(f\"\\nCost reduction: ${cost_reduction:,.2f}/hr ({cost_reduction/base_result['total_cost']*100:.2f}%)\")\n\n    # Find buses with largest LMP drops\n    lmp_changes = base_result['lmps'] - cf_result['lmps']\n    top_drops_idx = np.argsort(-lmp_changes)[:3]  # Top 3 drops\n\n    print(f\"\\nBuses with largest LMP reductions:\")\n    for idx in top_drops_idx:\n        bus_id = int(buses[idx][0])\n        base_lmp = base_result['lmps'][idx]\n        cf_lmp = cf_result['lmps'][idx]\n        delta = cf_lmp - base_lmp\n        print(f\"  Bus {bus_id}: ${base_lmp:.2f} -> ${cf_lmp:.2f} (Δ = ${delta:.2f}/MWh)\")\n\n    # Check if target line congestion is relieved\n    target_binding_in_base = any(\n        bl['from'] == target_from and bl['to'] == target_to\n        for bl in base_result['binding_lines']\n    )\n    target_binding_in_cf = any(\n        bl['from'] == target_from and bl['to'] == target_to\n        for bl in cf_result['binding_lines']\n    )\n\n    congestion_relieved = target_binding_in_base and not target_binding_in_cf\n\n    print(f\"\\nTarget line (Bus {target_from} -> {target_to}):\")\n    print(f\"  Binding in base case: {target_binding_in_base}\")\n    print(f\"  Binding in counterfactual: {target_binding_in_cf}\")\n    print(f\"  Congestion relieved: {congestion_relieved}\")\n\n    #
